+ "%lld items [App Intents]"
+ "App Intents App Protection unlock failed: %@"
+ "How many items Siri removed. %lld is the count"
+ "Moved %@ to the Trash, but couldn’t delete %@: %@ [App Intents]"
+ "Nothing was deleted. %@ can’t be moved to the Trash or deleted [App Intents]"
+ "Removed %@, but %@ can’t be moved to the Trash or deleted [App Intents]"
+ "Siri error when none of the items can be removed. %1$@ is a list of item names, %2$lld is how many"
+ "Siri error when some items were removed and others can’t be. %1$@ is the removed items (names or a count), %2$@ the items that can’t be removed, %3$lld how many can’t be removed"
+ "Siri error when some items were trashed but others couldn’t be deleted. %1$@ is the trashed items (names or a count), %2$@ the items not deleted, %3$@ the reason"
+ "These items aren’t available. Don’t look for them another way."
+ "These items need to be unlocked first."
